*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 Intern-S2-Preview 系列科学智能体基础模型，通过多模态预训练、统一后训练流水线（监督微调、多任务强化学习、智能体强化学习、在线策略蒸馏）及架构创新（时间序列建模扩展、Memory Decoder 记忆增强路径），实现了跨科学、多模态、智能体及通用基准的竞争性或领先性能，且无需修改 397B 主干即可快速适配专业领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现日益需要能够跨异构模态推理、与科学工具环境交互、并在长任务周期中持续进展的 AI 系统。现有模型在多模态科学理解、长时程推理及工具交互方面存在不足，亟需一种统一的智能体基础模型来支撑科学研究全流程。

Method: 1. 科学多模态预训练：利用渲染科学文档、交织图文数据及多元科学语料。2. 统一后训练流水线：监督微调 → 可扩展多任务强化学习 → 黑/白盒智能体强化学习 → 在线策略蒸馏。3. 训练稳定性与效率技术：部分回溯与离线策略修正、自适应长度正则化、在线投机解码、鲁棒多任务优化、轨迹感知经验组装。4. 架构创新：Intern-S2-Preview-397B 将时间序列建模从长序列理解扩展至数值预测；Memory Decoder 作为独立记忆增强路径，冻结 397B 主干实现快速领域专业化。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体及通用基准上均达到竞争性或领先水平。时间序列模块在 SciTS 基准上显著提升科学信号理解与预测能力。Intern-MemDec-4B 扩展模型在不修改冻结 397B 主干的前提下，将 Biology-Instructions 平均分从 56.92 提升至 60.32。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview 通过系统性的预训练、后训练流水线与架构设计，构建了具备多模态理解、长时程推理、工具交互及快速领域适配能力的科学智能体基础模型，为 AI 驱动的科学发现提供了强有力的基础设施。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [243] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SORT是一种稀疏谱框架，用于从噪声和不规则采样数据中学习正交基展开，应用于数据驱动的常微分方程发现，通过L1正则化回归直接估计展开系数，避免显式求积或解析内积计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的符号回归、基于语法的发现和SINDy风格的稀疏识别依赖于预定义的库，当库遗漏问题特定的非线性时模型会失效。SORT旨在提供一种紧凑的谱表示，将问题从脆弱的通用项选择转移到针对问题领域的基设计，并支持模型阶数一致的增长。

Method: SORT使用L1正则化回归直接从观测数据中估计正交基展开的系数，避免显式求积或解析内积计算。将向量场表示在选定的正交基中，学习稀疏系数展开。该方法适用于噪声和不规则采样数据。

Result: 在动力学系统实验中，SORT在基函数适应问题时匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线，在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配下表现出更稳定的退化。主导低阶系数随模型阶数增加而持久存在，支持阶数一致的模型增长。学习的展开还支持非线性逼近和高维积分估计。

Conclusion: SORT为系统辨识、逼近和积分提供了可复用的中间表示，使基设计成为科学建模问题的显式部分。它提供了一条互补路径，从紧凑谱表示出发，后续可引导搜索更简单的解析形式。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [244] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种干预感知的临床世界模型，用于心房颤动消融术后的复发风险预测。该模型将患者表示为结构化潜在状态，并利用基线影像、围手术期事件、生理测量等异步时间序列数据动态更新状态，实现了长期复发预测和瘢痕范围估计，且无需推理时的随访影像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型多将术后结果视为从基线到终点的一步映射，忽略了恢复过程中的不规则轨迹：临床观察、用药变更、再次干预、生理测量等异步记录会随时间改变风险评估。因此需要一种能建模术后动态演变的方法。

Method: 构建干预感知的临床世界模型：首先将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态；随后利用手术背景、静态协变量、经过时间、围事件生理嵌入等更新该状态；随访影像仅在训练阶段通过潜在预测目标提供监督；最终在心房颤动消融场景下应用，利用90天恢复窗口内的不规则记录预测长期复发风险。

Result: 在DECAAF-II数据集的重复内部交叉验证中，复发预测AUROC为0.756，AUPRC为0.777；瘢痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点，且推理时不需要随访MRI影像。学习到的状态支持不同时间跨度的复发风险查询，并允许对空白期记录进行回顾性编辑。

Conclusion: 该干预感知世界模型能有效捕捉术后动态恢复轨迹，利用异步事件序列提升长期预测性能，同时实现无需随访影像的瘢痕量化，为临床决策提供灵活的风险评估工具。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [245] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为'解掩增长复杂度'（UGC）的路径分辨数据几何度量，用于分析和优化离散扩散模型中的解掩过程。UGC的局部增量直接控制KL离散化误差，统一了伯努利子集和固定基数解掩方案。通过在对数揭示几率坐标下优化调度，并利用耦合揭示轨迹估计UGC增量，实现了认证最优采样器，在高概率下达到指定KL误差，迭代复杂度在常数因子内接近神谕过程。聚合UGC质量连接经典多变量依赖度量，细分区极限下平方根UGC密度的平方积分决定了最优欧拉离散化误差的主阶项。实验展示了显著的维度相关增益，包括通过常数数量的自适应分块实现Õ(√d)的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型的采样调度通常较为粗糙，未能充分利用数据几何结构，导致次优的离散化误差和计算效率。需要一种统一的理论框架来量化解掩过程中的数据几何，指导自适应调度设计，并提供可认证的最优性保证。

Method: 1. 引入解掩增长复杂度（UGC）作为路径分辨的数据几何度量，其局部增量直接界定KL离散化误差。2. 在对数揭示几率坐标系下推导优化的单块和多块调度。3. 通过耦合揭示轨迹上的KL增量从样本中估计UGC增量。4. 构建认证最优采样器，在高概率下达到指定KL误差。5. 分析聚合UGC质量与经典依赖度量的联系，以及细分区极限下的最优欧拉离散化误差。

Result: 理论上：统一了伯努利子集和固定基数解掩方案的分析；证明认证最优采样器的迭代复杂度在常数因子内接近神谕；建立UGC与经典多变量依赖度量的联系；推导细分区极限下的最优误差主阶项。实验上：展示显著的维度相关增益，自适应分块调度在常数分块数下实现Õ(√d)的改进。

Conclusion: UGC为离散扩散采样提供了统一的几何分析框架，实现了自适应调度的理论最优性和实践高效性。该工作为离散生成建模中的计算资源分配提供了原则性指导，并揭示了数据几何与采样复杂度的深层联系。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [246] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vero是首个用于评估仓库级联合实现与证明合成的基准，包含43个来自真实仓库的多模块实例，涵盖多种语言和领域，测试显示最强代理仅解决27/43实例，且在最难仓库上无解，揭示当前代理在仓库级验证软件合成上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理生成代码无正确性保证，验证代码生成要求代理同时产出实现和机器检查证明，但现有基准仅关注单个函数或给定实现下的证明生成，缺乏对真实多模块代码库中连贯实现与证明选择的评估，因此需要一个仓库级基准来填补空白。

Method: 构建Vero基准，收集43个多模块实例，源自真实仓库（Python, Dafny, Verus, Coq），领域涵盖密码协议到分布式系统；每个实例包含预定义API接口、人工策划形式化规范、参考实现的Lean 4多模块仓库，支持纯证明和代码加证明两种评估模式；引入审计机制，允许代理形式化证明规范不可满足或参考代码错误，以发现并修正潜在错误；使用前沿编码代理配置并接入Lean工具链进行评估。

Result: 最强代理仅完全解决43个实例中的27个，在最难仓库上无任何规范闭合；当前代理在仓库级验证软件合成上仍显不足。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量仓库级验证软件合成进展提供了具体测试平台，揭示现有代理仍远未达到要求；发布了基准、策划流程和评估工具。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [247] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree 是一种无需训练的推测解码方法，将预训练的自回归（AR）校正头从链式结构扩展到树式结构，通过批量构建固定宽度的候选树并使用最佳优先剪枝选择验证树，解耦了 AR 头推理与顺序堆操作，实现了最高的平均接受长度和加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的起草器虽能并行预测整个 token 块降低提议延迟，但其逐位置分布是边缘分布而非条件于每条起草路径上已选 token；现有循环校正仅在单条起草链上融合因果信息，而基于扩散的树构建虽扩大了候选覆盖，却未将校正沿各分支传播。因此需要一种方法在树结构上保留 AR 校正的因果条件能力，同时保持并行构建的高效性。

Method: DARTree 首先在单批次中逐层扩展并为所有节点打分，构建固定宽度的候选树；随后仅对该候选树应用最佳优先剪枝得到验证树，将 AR 头的推理与顺序堆操作解耦。该方法无需额外训练，直接复用预训练的 AR 校正头。

Result: 在七个数学、代码和聊天基准上，DARTree 在所有四种模型-温度配置中均达到最高平均接受长度和加速比；单次验证轮最多接受 12.97 个 token，比 DFlash 多 98.6%，比 Domino 多 27.9%；本地测量的无损加速比最高达 9.73×。

Conclusion: DARTree 通过将 AR 校正头扩展到树结构并采用批量构建与最佳优先剪枝，成功结合了扩散起草器的并行性与 AR 校正的因果条件能力，显著提升了推测解码的接受长度和推理速度，为无损加速自回归语言模型提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [248] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 Jaccard 分数（IoU）在多标签分类和二值分割中的校准性与代理损失维度，证明精确校准需要指数维度，而固定加性遗憾容忍下存在多项式维度的近似代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多标签分类中 Jaccard 分数的精确校准问题，揭示精确校准凸代理损失的维度下界，并寻找多项式维度的近似代理以实现可控的遗憾界。

Method: 结合有限 MinHash Gram 表示与布尔莫比乌斯反演，证明 Jaccard、偏移损失和普通损失矩阵非奇异且损失列仿射维数为 2^s-1；利用阶乘加权分布建立校准复杂度下界；构造 F1 到 Jaccard 的遗憾转移及基于 MinHash 的平方损失代理。

Result: 精确校准的校准复杂度满足 2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim(L^Jac) ≤ 2^s-1，精确校准需指数维度；提出两种多项式维度近似：F1-to-Jaccard 转移实现渐近 Jaccard 惜 ≤ 3-2√2，MinHash 平方损失代理对任意 α>0, ρ∈(0,1) 达到 Jaccard 惜下界 α，维度分别为 O((s^2+s log(1/ρ))/α^2) 和 O((s+log(1/ρ))/α^2)。

Conclusion: 零遗憾精确校准需要指数预测维度，但任何固定的加性遗憾容忍度都允许存在多项式维度的预测代理，为实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [249] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Defensive Booster的简单防御性预测算法，同时实现了在线梯度提升和在线弱到强提升的两种不可比保证：在每个自适应序列上，其Brier分数与由弱假设类H的张量诱导的最佳预测器竞争；同时，当实现的转录满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier分数和随机分类错误率满足与在线分类提升相同的速率保证。算法仅需访问一个弱类学习器，效率极高，实验显示预测性能强且运行时间比基线快几个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升技术分别提供两种互不相容的保证：在线梯度提升在每个序列上与H的张量诱导的最佳预测器在Brier分数上竞争，但当张量不包含准确预测器时无保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下将分类错误率降至零，但条件失效时保证很少。本文旨在高效地同时获得这两种保证。

Method: 提出Defensive Booster算法，基于提升的“对偶视角”：当算法的随机分类错误率持续较高时，其错误权重形成平滑重加权，使得每个弱假设的优势较低，从而产生事后硬核证书证明弱学习条件失效。算法仅维护一个弱类学习器，并开发了强自适应变体，在每个时间区间同时满足两种保证。

Result: Defensive Booster在每个自适应序列上以与在线梯度提升相同的速率在Brier分数上竞争；当满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier分数和随机分类错误率达到与在线分类提升相同的速率保证。实验在合成和真实数据流上显示强预测性能（有时大幅优于所有先前基线），且运行时间快几个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster通过简单高效的单一弱学习器访问，统一了在线梯度提升和在线弱到强提升的优势，提供了同时竞争Brier分数和弱学习条件下的错误率保证，且具有强自适应性和显著的计算效率提升。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [Knowledge Synthesis Review Framework: Task-Level Benchmarking of LLM-Based Systems for Multi-Source Evidence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12741)
*Wafa Shafqat,Mark Patterson,Steven N. Liss*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KSR框架将证据综合分解为筛选、提取、分析、综合四个任务，基准测试多个LLM系统，并将任务路由至最佳系统，在专家持续验证下应用于全量语料库，揭示了跨源不对称和盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速演变领域的证据碎片化分布于学术研究、行业报告、政策文件和媒体源中，质量、结构、目的各异，难以及时综合；LLM虽可能加速但跨认知任务的可靠性未知。

Method: 提出人在回路的KSR框架，将证据综合分解为筛选、提取、分析、综合四个任务；在244文档基准子集（从1,893文档语料库抽取，涵盖四类来源）上对GPT-5、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro、NotebookLM进行基准测试，参照高一致性专家金标准（92.2%一致，kappa=0.80）；任务路由至表现最佳系统，持续专家验证；污染检查排除训练数据泄露；最终应用于全量语料库。

Result: 无单一系统全任务领先。Claude Sonnet 4筛选准确率最高(82.8%)，GPT-5召回率最高(91.8%)但特异性较低。提取任务标题和来源一致性超90%，但作者和参考文献字段下降。解释性分析和跨源综合表现下降最明显，仍需专家判断。污染检查无证据显示先验曝光虚高结果。全量语料库应用揭示跨源不对称和盲点：工人福祉、小型企业、全球南方。

Conclusion: KSR提供透明、可审计、模型无关的框架，用于治理LLM在研究综合中的辅助作用，同时保持人类问责制。

Abstract: Evidence in rapidly evolving fields is fragmented across academic studies, industry reports, policy documents, and media sources that differ in quality, structure, and purpose, making timely synthesis difficult. Large language models (LLMs) may accelerate this work, but their reliability across the distinct cognitive tasks of a review remains uncertain. We introduce the Knowledge Synthesis Review (KSR), a human-in-the-loop framework that decomposes evidence synthesis into screening, extraction, analysis, and synthesis, benchmarks LLM-based systems on each task against expert reference standards, and routes each task to the best-performing system under continuous expert validation. We evaluated GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 4, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and NotebookLM on a 244-document benchmark subset drawn from a 1,893-document corpus on AI and work spanning four source types, against a gold standard with high inter-rater reliability (92.2% agreement, kappa = 0.80). No system led on all tasks. Claude Sonnet 4 achieved the highest screening accuracy (82.8%) and GPT-5 the highest recall (91.8%) at the expense of lower specificity. Extraction exceeded 90% agreement for titles and sources but degraded in author and reference fields. Performance declined most in interpretive analysis and cross-source synthesis, where expert judgment remained essential. A contamination check on post-cutoff documents showed no evidence that prior exposure inflated results. Applied to the full corpus, the routed workflow surfaced cross-source asymmetries and blind spots that single-source synthesis would miss, including worker well-being, small firms, and the Global South. KSR offers a transparent, auditable, model-agnostic framework for governing LLM assistance in research synthesis while preserving human accountability.

</details>


### [251] [DrEM: Dual-Side Robust Ensemble Ranking from Noisy User Preference Predictions in Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12778)
*Canwei Huang,Tiantian He,Xiaoxiao Xu,Jun Zhang,Ziran Deng,Weike Pan,Chunjie Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DrEM，一种双边鲁棒的集成排序框架，用于解决工业视频推荐系统中上游多任务模型输出的多维用户偏好预测（pxtrs）存在的预测噪声问题，该噪声会同时污染监督信号和特征输入，导致排序性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视频推荐系统通常采用多阶段架构，在集成排序阶段，上游多任务模型输出的多维用户偏好预测（pxtrs）被融合为统一排序分数以反映用户满意度。由于真实满意度难以直接观测，现有方法常将 pxtrs 同时作为输入特征和代理偏好的构建来源。然而，pxtrs 作为上游预测模型的输出，必然包含预测噪声，这种噪声会双向传播：监督侧噪声可能翻转代理偏好并引入错误梯度；特征侧噪声可能通过模型输入传播并破坏排序分数的稳定性。现有集成排序方法通常将 pxtrs 视为可靠信号，忽略了预测噪声的影响。

Method: 提出 DrEM 框架，包含两大核心组件：1) 风险去噪鲁棒损失：利用估计的偏好翻转概率校正经验风险，从监督侧减轻噪声标签的影响；2) 偏好保持排序一致性正则化器：从预测噪声分布中采样扰动，约束模型输出在扰动下的排序一致性，从特征侧提升输出稳定性。理论上给出了预测噪声的近似分布，并证明了在翻转概率估计误差下鲁棒损失仍保持优势。

Result: 大量离线实验和大规模在线 A/B 测试验证了 DrEM 的有效性和鲁棒性，显著提升了集成排序阶段的性能和稳定性。

Conclusion: DrEM 通过双边鲁棒设计（监督侧风险去噪 + 特征侧一致性正则），系统性地解决了上游预测噪声在集成排序阶段的双向传播问题，为工业推荐系统的排序阶段提供了更可靠的噪声鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial video recommendation systems typically adopt a multi-stage architecture. At the ensemble ranking stage, multi-dimensional user preference predictions (pxtrs) from an upstream multi-task model are fused into a unified ranking score to reflect user satisfaction. Since users' true satisfaction is difficult to observe directly, ensemble ranking models commonly use pxtrs both as input features and as a source for constructing proxy preferences. However, as outputs of an upstream prediction model, pxtrs inevitably contain prediction noise, which propagates to downstream learning across two sides. On the supervision side, noisy pxtrs may flip proxy preferences and introduce erroneous gradients. On the feature side, pxtr noise may propagate through model inputs and destabilize ranking scores. Existing ensemble ranking methods typically treat pxtrs as reliable signals and overlook such prediction noise. To address this, we propose DrEM, a dual-side robust ensemble ranking framework. Our DrEM introduces a risk-denoising robust loss that corrects the empirical risk using estimated preference flip probability. Meanwhile, it samples perturbations from the distribution of prediction noise and introduces a preference-preserving ranking consistency regularizer to improve feature-side output stability. Theoretically, we obtain an approximate distribution of the prediction noise and prove that the robust loss remains superior under flip probability estimation error. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our DrEM.

</details>


### [252] [A Comprehensive Empirical Evaluation of Vector Database Systems for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search: Performance, Quality, and Resource Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12812)
*Ashen Rashmiks,Tiroshan Madushanka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文对七个主流向量数据库系统进行了系统性实证评估，涵盖检索质量、查询延迟、吞吐量和资源利用率等15项指标，基于六个多样化数据集（超400万向量），得出各系统在不同场景下的优劣表现，并提供选型指南和开源基准测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管向量数据库在RAG、语义搜索、推荐系统等现代AI应用中至关重要，但缺乏综合、可复现的基准测试来联合评估检索质量、查询延迟、吞吐量和资源利用率，因此需要系统性评估以指导实践者选型。

Method: 选取七个主流向量数据库（FAISS、Qdrant、Milvus、Weaviate、Chroma、pgvector、LanceDB），使用六个多样化数据集（SIFT、GIST、MS MARCO、GloVe等），向量规模超400万、维度96-960，测量15项指标（Recall@K、Precision@K、MRR、NDCG@K、Hit Rate@K、延迟百分位、QPS、冷启动延迟、索引构建时间、内存、存储等），进行全面实证对比。

Result: 在SIFT1M上，FAISS单节点吞吐最高（866 QPS）但缺乏数据库运维功能；Weaviate开箱即用召回率最佳（>99%）；Qdrant在完整数据库中延迟最低（中位数4.55 ms）；LanceDB以牺牲检索质量换取显著更快的索引构建速度。

Conclusion: 论文为实践者提供了系统选型指南，并将基准测试框架开源，填补了向量数据库联合评估的空白，有助于推动向量数据库在AI应用中的合理选型和性能优化。

Abstract: Vector databases have emerged as critical infrastructure for modern artificial intelligence applications, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), semantic search, and recommendation systems. Despite their growing importance, there remains a significant gap in comprehensive, reproducible benchmarks that jointly evaluate retrieval quality, query latency, throughput, and resource utilization. We present a systematic empirical evaluation of seven prominent vector database systems: FAISS, Qdrant, Milvus, Weaviate, Chroma, pgvector, and LanceDB. Our methodology spans six diverse datasets, from classical computer-vision descriptors (SIFT, GIST) to transformer-based text embeddings (MS MARCO, GloVe), encompassing over 4 million vectors at dimensionalities from 96 to 960. We measure 15 metrics spanning retrieval quality (Recall@K, Precision@K, MRR, NDCG@K, Hit Rate@K), query performance (latency percentiles, QPS, cold-start latency), and resource consumption (index build time, memory, storage). On SIFT1M, FAISS achieves the highest single-node throughput (866 QPS) but lacks database operational features; Weaviate provides the best out-of-the-box recall (> 99%); Qdrant offers the best latency among full databases (4.55~ms median); and LanceDB trades retrieval quality for substantially faster index construction. We derive system-selection guidelines for practitioners and release our benchmarking framework as open-source software.

</details>


### [253] [Query Translation vs. Cross-Lingual Embeddings for Sinhala-Tamil E-Government Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12820)
*Dharshi Balasubramaniyam,Tiroshan Madushanka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对比评估了跨语言信息检索（CLIR）方法，用于用僧伽罗语和泰米尔语查询检索英语政府信息。实验表明，多语言嵌入模型（BGE-M3）显著优于查询翻译方法，在低资源政府领域提供更有效且可扩展的跨语言RAG解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低资源语言（僧伽罗语、泰米尔语）查询英语政府信息的跨语言检索问题，单语检索效果差，需探索更有效的CLIR范式。

Method: 采用两种CLIR范式：查询翻译（QT）使用Google Translate、NLLB、mBART50；跨语言嵌入（CLE）使用LaBSE、多语言E5、BGE-M3。基准为单语英语检索。在人工验证的基准上实验，包含500个僧伽罗语、泰米尔语、英语问答对，源自斯里兰卡政府信息中心的1,699个分段上下文。评估指标为Recall@k (k=1,3,5,10,15)。

Result: 单语检索Recall@15 <10%，所有CLIR方法大幅提升。BGE-M3达到最高Recall@15：僧伽罗语-英语96.2%，泰米尔语-英语95.6%，优于最佳QT（Google Translate：92.4%和93.0%），且避免了翻译开销。

Conclusion: 多语言嵌入模型为低资源政府领域的跨语言检索增强生成（RAG）提供了更有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative evaluation of cross-lingual information retrieval (CLIR) methods for retrieving English government information using Sinhala and Tamil queries. Two CLIR paradigms are investigated: Query Translation (QT), employing Google Translate, NLLB, and mBART50, and Cross-Lingual Embeddings (CLE), using LaBSE, multilingual E5, and BGE-M3, with monolingual English retrieval as the baseline. Experiments are conducted on a human-verified benchmark comprising 500 Sinhala, Tamil, and English question-answer pairs derived from 1,699 segmented contexts from Sri Lanka's Government Information Center (GIC). Retrieval performance is evaluated using Recall@k (k = 1, 3, 5, 10, 15). Monolingual retrieval performs poorly (Recall@15 <10%), whereas all CLIR approaches substantially improve retrieval accuracy. Among them, BGE-M3 achieves the highest Recall@15, reaching 96.2% for Sinhala-English and 95.6% for Tamil-English, outperforming the best QT approach (Google Translate: 92.4% and 93.0%) while avoiding translation overhead. These results demonstrate that multilingual embedding models provide a more effective and scalable solution for cross-lingual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in low-resource government domains.

</details>


### [254] [FSGR: Mitigating Token Frequency Bias for Fair SID-Based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12845)
*Yuchen Zheng,Sihan Xu,Jingwen Yang,Xiangrui Cai,Haiwei Zhang,Xiaojie Yuan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发现基于语义ID（SID）的生成式推荐中存在“Token Frequency Bias”公平性问题，即高频SID token被系统性过度预测、低频token欠预测。为解决此问题，提出FSGR框架：SID构建阶段采用基于最优传输的分配优化和双准则重锚定机制，训练阶段采用两阶段训练策略及层级频率校准。在三个公开数据集上，FSGR将基尼公平性指标平均提升20%以上，同时保持推荐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SID方法主要关注码本质量，忽视了token频率不平衡对下游推荐公平性的影响。该偏差源于SID构建时语义码本不平衡、推荐训练时流行度偏差与最大似然估计目标的共同作用，导致不同商品类别曝光不公。直接应用LLM去偏方法因SID token的层级语义特性而效果次优。

Method: 提出FSGR公平性优化框架：1）SID构建阶段：基于最优传输的分配优化和双准则重锚定机制，构建更平衡的SID表示空间。2）推荐训练阶段：两阶段训练策略，引入层级频率校准进行层级特定的公平性微调。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，使用三种主干模型实验表明，FSGR有效缓解了token频率偏差，基尼公平性指标平均提升超过20%，同时保持了有竞争力的推荐准确性。

Conclusion: FSGR框架通过在SID构建和推荐训练两个阶段分别引入平衡机制与层级校准，成功解决了SID生成式推荐中的token频率偏差问题，显著提升了推荐公平性而不牺牲准确性。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID)-based generative recommendation has recently achieved remarkable success. However, existing methods suffer from a previously overlooked fairness issue, which we term \textbf{Token Frequency Bias}, where high-frequency SID tokens are systematically over-predicted while low-frequency SID tokens are under-predicted. This bias originates from the combined effects of imbalanced semantic codebooks during SID construction, and popularity bias together with the maximum likelihood estimation objective during recommendation training, resulting in unfair exposure across item categories. Existing SID methods mainly focus on improving codebook quality and overlook the impact of token frequency imbalance on downstream recommendation fairness, while LLM debiasing methods often yield suboptimal results when directly applied to SID-based recommendation, due to the hierarchical semantics of SID tokens. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{FSGR}, a fairness optimization framework for SID-based generative recommendation. During SID construction, FSGR employs OT-based Assignment Optimization and Dual-Criteria Re-anchor mechanism to form a more balanced SID representation space. During recommendation training, it adopts a two-stage training strategy and introduces Hierarchical Frequency Calibration for layer-specific fairness fine-tuning. Experiments on three public datasets with three backbone models demonstrate that FSGR mitigates token frequency bias and delivers an average Gini fairness improvement of over 20\% while maintaining competitive recommendation accuracy.

</details>


### [255] [STAR: Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation for PCVR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12986)
*Yimeng Xu,Haorui Zhang,Yingqi Song,Ying Jiang,Lan Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: STAR (Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation) 是针对 KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge 提出的后点击转化率（PCVR）预测框架，结合结构化特征分词与目标感知兴趣表达，在 HyFormer 风格多序列骨干网络之上引入高基数信号恢复、显式用户-物品交互 Token、目标感知序列解码及加权用户-物品对比辅助目标（InfoNCE 启发），并对齐训练/推理管线。消融实验显示时序上下文贡献最大，对比对齐、目标感知兴趣编码、高基数序列特征恢复提供次要但有益提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统的 PCVR 预测需联合建模异构非序列特征、多行为用户序列与目标物品感知兴趣，同时应对高基数稀疏特征、缺失值及训练-推理不一致等挑战。现有方法在结构化特征表示、目标感知兴趣建模及训练推理对齐方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 基于 HyFormer 多序列骨干，提出 STAR 框架：1) 结构化特征分词处理异构特征；2) 高基数信号恢复机制；3) 引入显式用户-物品交互 Token；4) 目标感知序列解码建模目标物品感知兴趣；5) 设计加权用户-物品对比辅助目标（InfoNCE 变体）；6) 通过重建特征重映射表与结构超参数对齐训练推理管线。

Result: 挑战数据集实验表明：时序上下文带来 AUC 最大提升；对比对齐、目标感知兴趣编码、高基数序列特征恢复提供较小但稳定的增益；LogLoss 作为校准诊断指标报告。各组件对排名 AUC 的可靠改进程度已通过消融研究量化。

Conclusion: STAR 通过结构化分词、目标感知兴趣表示及多项工程增强，有效解决了 PCVR 预测中的异构特征建模、高基数稀疏性、目标感知兴趣提取及训推一致性问题，为工业级推荐排序提供了实用且可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (PCVR) prediction is a core ranking task in industrial recommender systems. Modern ranking models must jointly capture heterogeneous non-sequential features, multi-behavior user sequences, and target-item-aware user interests, while remaining robust to high-cardinality sparse features, missing values, and train-inference inconsistencies. In this paper, we present STAR (Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation), a practical framework for the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge. STAR combines structured feature tokenization with target-aware interest representation on top of a HyFormer-style multi-sequence backbone. It introduces high-cardinality signal recovery, explicit user-item interaction tokens, target-aware sequence decoding, and a weighted user-item contrastive auxiliary objective inspired by InfoNCE. We further align the training and inference pipelines by reconstructing feature remapping tables and structural hyperparameters from the saved training configuration. Experiments on the challenge dataset identify the components that most reliably improve ranking AUC, while LogLoss is reported as a calibration diagnostic. The main ablation study shows a large gain from temporal context, with smaller but useful contributions from contrastive alignment, target-aware interest encoding, and high-cardinality sequence feature recovery.

</details>


### [256] [Generative Universal Multimodal Retrieval with Dual-role Identifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12987)
*Kaipeng Li,Haitao Yu,Xuanchen Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 DrIG，一种用于通用多模态检索的生成式框架，采用双角色标识符（顺序角色与集合角色）解决前缀错误、单模态局限及离散标识符精度不足等问题，在 M-BEIR 等基准上超越现有生成式多模态基线，混合重排序实现高效率与高效果的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式信息检索（GIR）面临三大挑战：1）受限的从左到右解码易受前缀级错误和局部最优影响；2）多数研究局限于单模态，跨文本、图像及图文混合模态的指令感知检索未被充分探索；3）基于离散标识符的 GIR 虽效率高，但检索精度仍落后于稠密向量检索方法。

Method: 提出 DrIG 框架，为每个候选项分配一个残差量化标识符，赋予双重互补角色：顺序角色中自回归解码，首 token 显式建模模态，后续 token 逐步捕捉细粒度语义；集合角色中将相同 token 重解释为无序集合，提供与前缀无关的相关性先验，指导受限束搜索并缓解局部最优错误。支持多模态、多任务、多领域的统一检索。

Result: 在 M-BEIR 基准及文生图评测集上实验表明：1）DrIG 在多样化任务上持续超越最先进的生成式多模态基线，混合重排序在强稠密检索器上实现有利的效率-效果权衡；2）消融与扩展分析揭示基础 LMM、束大小、重排序深度、融合策略对检索性能的影响，为系统设计提供实用指导。

Conclusion: DrIG 通过双角色标识符有效解决了生成式多模态检索中的前缀错误、模态局限及精度瓶颈，实现了跨模态统一检索的新范式，且混合重排序策略兼顾了效率与效果，为大规模多模态检索系统提供了可落地的设计参考。

Abstract: Generative information retrieval (GIR) has emerged as a compelling alternative to the conventional index-retrieve-then-rank retrieval pipeline by training a generator to produce the identifiers of relevant items directly. Despite its promise, a number of open challenges still remain. First, constrained left-to-right decoding is vulnerable to prefix-level errors and local optima. Second, most prior GIR research remains largely unimodal, leaving instruction-aware retrieval across text, image, and mixed image-text items underexplored. Third, although discrete identifier-based GIR offers higher efficiency, its retrieval accuracy still lags behind that of the cutting-edge dense-vector-based retrieval methods. Motivated by these challenges, we propose DrIG, a novel Generative framework for universal multimodal retrieval featuring Dual-role Identifiers, which supports diverse retrieval tasks across multiple modalities and domains. Each candidate is assigned a single residual-quantized identifier that serves two complementary roles. In its sequential role, the identifier is decoded autoregressively, where the first token explicitly models modality and the remaining tokens capture progressively finer semantics. In its set-based role, the same tokens are reinterpreted as an unordered set to provide a prefix-independent relevance prior, which guides constrained beam search and alleviates local-optimum errors. Extensive experiments on the M-BEIR benchmark and the text-to-image evaluation datasets show that:(1)DrIG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative multimodal baselines across diverse tasks, while hybrid reranking achieves a favorable efficiency-effectiveness trade-off against strong dense retrievers. (2)Ablation and scaling analyses reveal how the base LMM, beam size, reranking depth, and fusion strategy affect retrieval performance, providing practical guidance for system design.

</details>


### [257] [When Should Multi-Round RAG Stop? Structured Stopping Judgments and Retrieval Reduction in Search-R1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13237)
*Weimeng Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于S2G-RAG的结构化判断器，在冻结的Search-R1流水线上训练Qwen3.5-2B模型，用于多轮检索增强生成中的停止决策，实现了检索调用减少3.70%，同时基本保持答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮RAG需要决定何时停止搜索，这是一个序列选择问题而非独立状态分类任务，现有方法可能不够高效，需要一种能在证据积累过程中做出停止决策的结构化判断方法。

Method: 将S2G-RAG的结构化充分性与差距判断适配到冻结的Search-R1流水线，在900个不相交HotpotQA问题的3009个状态上训练Qwen3.5-2B判断器，保持推理器、检索器、语料库、提示和搜索预算不变，在分组验证集上选择判断器检查点和停止阈值并冻结，最后在确认性测试集上评估。

Result: 与原生Search-R1相比，检索调用减少77次（3.70%），官方精确匹配下降0.625个百分点。

Conclusion: 训练的S2G风格结构化判断器在基本保持答案准确性的同时减少了检索，但结果不意味着准确性未变或提升、安全停止或总推理成本降低。

Abstract: Multi-round retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) must decide when to stop searching as evidence accumulates. Because the deployed policy is determined by the first STOP on each trajectory, this is a sequential selection problem rather than an independent state-classification task. We adapt S2G-RAG's structured sufficiency-and-gap judgment to a frozen Search-R1 pipeline and train a Qwen3.5-2B judge on 3,009 states from 900 disjoint HotpotQA questions. Search-R1's reasoner, retriever, corpus, prompt, and search budget remain unchanged, while the judge checkpoint and stopping threshold are selected on grouped validation and frozen before confirmatory evaluation. On the confirmatory test set, the resulting policy reduces retrieval calls by 77 (3.70\%) relative to Native Search-R1, while Official Exact Match decreases by 0.625 percentage points. Thus, the trained S2G-style structured judge reduces retrieval while broadly preserving answer accuracy. The result does not imply unchanged or improved accuracy, safe stopping, or lower total inference cost.

</details>


### [258] [Structure then Query: Enabling Precise Analytical Queries over Unstructured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13384)
*Teng Lin,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AnnoIndex提出了一种面向非结构化文档的结构化分析系统，包含Annotation Index和Structured Query Engine两大核心组件，将非结构化文本转化为结构化索引，实现低成本精确过滤与推理，在三个真实数据集上达到最高平均F1 0.87，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化文档占企业与Web数据大多数，现有检索方法仍依赖向量相似度的模糊匹配，导致准确获取信息、进行结构化分析与推理极具挑战。

Method: 1. Annotation Index：利用SchemaLoop自动从语料构建分层标注模式，再用轻量语言模型抽取具体数值，将分散文本物化为结构化索引，规避向量相似度黑盒匹配，将属性抽取成本从在线查询摊销至一次性构建。2. Structured Query Engine：将用户问题编译为基于SQL扩展的执行计划，先用Annotation Index精确过滤文档，再按成本升序渐进应用抽取操作，仅对极少需深度语义理解的语料调用LLM，抽取结果回写索引以降低后续查询成本。

Result: 在三个真实数据集实验中，AnnoIndex持续超越最先进基线，取得最高平均F1 0.87，并在复杂多跳连接与渐进推理查询上保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: AnnoIndex通过构建结构化标注索引与分级查询引擎，解决了向量相似度检索的精度与推理局限，实现了非结构化文档的低成本、高精度结构化分析。

Abstract: Unstructured documents constitute the majority of enterprise and web data. With the rapid development of large language models(LLMs), researchers have started to build data systems that analyze unstructured textual documents like operating on databases. However, because mainstream retrieval methods still relies on fuzzy matching based on vector similarity, accurately obtaining information and performing structured analysis and reasoning remains a major challenge. To address these limitations, AnnoIndex introduces two core fundamental components. The first is Annotation Index. The system uses a module called SchemaLoop to automatically create hierarchical annotation schemas from the raw corpus, and then uses lightweight language model to extract specific values. It turns scattered unstructured text into a materialized, structured index that enables low-cost filtering and querying. The annotation index avoids the black-box matching of vector similarity and amortizes attribute extraction costs from online queries to a one-time build. The second innovation is a Structured Query Engine. It compiles user questions into execution plans based on SQL extension. It first uses the Annotation Index for precise documents filtering, then gradually applies extraction operations in ascending order of cost, resorting to LLMs only for the remaining minimal fraction of the corpus that require deep semantic understanding. The extracted attributions are merged into the annotation index, reducing the cost of future queries. Experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that AnnoIndex consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the highest average F1 score (0.87) while maintaining robust performance on complex multi-hop join and progressive reasoning queries.

</details>
